# MateryalPH Data Dictionary

> Generated by `php artisan materyalph:schema-document` from the fully migrated PostgreSQL `public` schema. Do not edit generated table definitions by hand.

All `*_centavos` columns store integer Philippine centavos. All `timestamp with time zone` values are persisted in UTC and business deadlines are rendered in Asia/Manila.

## `analytics_refresh_runs`

MAT-01–MAT-07 comparability, observation, publication, or audience-safe aggregate record.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `kind` | `character varying` | No | — | Kind. |
| `environment` | `character varying` | No | `'TEST'::character varying` | Environment. |
| `dataset_id` | `character varying` | No | — | Dataset id. |
| `snapshot_date` | `date` | No | — | Snapshot date. |
| `revision` | `integer` | No | `1` | Revision. |
| `state` | `character varying` | No | `'RUNNING'::character varying` | State. |
| `observed_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Observed at. |
| `source_count` | `integer` | No | `0` | Source count. |
| `selected_count` | `integer` | No | `0` | Selected count. |
| `content_hash` | `character varying` | Yes | — | Content hash. |
| `supersedes_run_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Supersedes run id. |
| `reviewer_reason` | `text` | Yes | — | Reviewer reason. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |
| `error_code` | `character varying` | Yes | — | Error code. |
| `validated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Validated at. |
| `published_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Published at. |

**Constraints**

- `analytics_refresh_counts_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (selected_count <= source_count AND (state::text <> 'FAILED'::text OR error_code IS NOT NULL) AND (state::text <> 'VALIDATED'::text OR validated_at IS NOT NULL) AND (state::text <> 'PUBLISHED'::text OR published_at IS NOT NULL))`
- `analytics_refresh_environment_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (environment::text = ANY (ARRAY['TEST'::character varying, 'DEMO'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `analytics_refresh_kind_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (kind::text = 'MATERIAL_PRICE_DAILY'::text)`
- `analytics_refresh_state_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (state::text = ANY (ARRAY['RUNNING'::character varying, 'VALIDATED'::character varying, 'PUBLISHED'::character varying, 'FAILED'::character varying, 'SUPERSEDED'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `analytics_refresh_runs_supersedes_run_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (supersedes_run_id) REFERENCES analytics_refresh_runs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `analytics_refresh_runs_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `analytics_refresh_runs_kind_environment_dataset_id_snapshot_dat` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (kind, environment, dataset_id, snapshot_date, revision)`

**Indexes**

- `analytics_refresh_runs_kind_environment_dataset_id_snapshot_dat` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX analytics_refresh_runs_kind_environment_dataset_id_snapshot_dat ON public.analytics_refresh_runs USING btree (kind, environment, dataset_id, snapshot_date, revision)`
- `analytics_refresh_runs_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX analytics_refresh_runs_pkey ON public.analytics_refresh_runs USING btree (id)`

## `fee_assessments`

FIN-01–FIN-12 financial control and evidence record.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `order_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Order id. |
| `vendor_organization_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Vendor organization id. |
| `financial_snapshot_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Financial snapshot id. |
| `fee_policy_version_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Fee policy version id. |
| `commission_basis_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Commission basis centavos. |
| `commission_basis_points` | `integer` | No | — | Commission basis points. |
| `earned_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Earned centavos. |
| `earned_target_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Earned target centavos. |
| `credited_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Credited centavos. |
| `credited_state` | `character varying` | No | `'NOT_CREDITED'::character varying` | Credited state. |
| `state` | `character varying` | No | `'ESTIMATED'::character varying` | State. |
| `earned_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Earned at. |
| `dispute_hold` | `boolean` | No | `false` | Dispute hold. |
| `dispute_hold_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Dispute hold at. |
| `calculation_hash` | `character varying` | No | — | Calculation hash. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |

**Constraints**

- `fee_assessment_amounts_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (commission_basis_centavos >= 0 AND commission_basis_points = 200 AND earned_centavos >= 0 AND earned_target_centavos >= 0 AND credited_centavos >= 0 AND credited_centavos <= earned_centavos)`
- `fee_assessment_state_check` — CHECK: `CHECK ((state::text = ANY (ARRAY['ESTIMATED'::character varying, 'EARNED'::character varying, 'CANCELLED'::character varying]::text[])) AND (credited_state::text = ANY (ARRAY['NOT_CREDITED'::character varying, 'PARTIALLY_CREDITED'::character varying, 'CREDITED'::character varying]::text[])))`
- `fee_assessments_fee_policy_version_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (fee_policy_version_id) REFERENCES fee_policy_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`

- `fee_assessments_order_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `fee_assessments_vendor_organization_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (vendor_organization_id) REFERENCES vendor_organizations(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `fee_assessments_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `fee_assessments_order_id_fee_policy_version_id_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (order_id, fee_policy_version_id)`

- `fee_assessments_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX fee_assessments_pkey ON public.fee_assessments USING btree (id)`

## `fee_statements`

FIN-01–FIN-12 financial control and evidence record.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `vendor_organization_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Vendor organization id. |
| `environment` | `character varying` | No | `'TEST'::character varying` | Environment. |
| `period_start` | `date` | No | — | Period start. |
| `period_end` | `date` | No | — | Period end. |
| `issued_on` | `date` | No | — | Issued on. |
| `due_on` | `date` | No | — | Due on. |
| `charges_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Charges centavos. |
| `credits_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Credits centavos. |
| `paid_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Paid centavos. |
| `balance_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Balance centavos. |
| `outstanding_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Outstanding centavos. |
| `state` | `character varying` | No | `'DRAFT'::character varying` | State. |
| `version` | `integer` | No | `1` | Version. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |

**Constraints**

- `fee_statement_amounts_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (charges_centavos >= 0 AND credits_centavos >= 0 AND paid_centavos >= 0 AND balance_centavos >= 0 AND outstanding_centavos = balance_centavos AND balance_centavos = (charges_centavos - credits_centavos - paid_centavos))`
- `fee_statement_dates_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (period_start <= period_end AND issued_on <= due_on)`
- `fee_statement_state_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (state::text = ANY (ARRAY['DRAFT'::character varying, 'ISSUED'::character varying, 'PARTIALLY_PAID'::character varying, 'PAID'::character varying, 'VOIDED'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `fee_statements_vendor_organization_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (vendor_organization_id) REFERENCES vendor_organizations(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `fee_statements_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `fee_statements_vendor_organization_id_period_start_period_end_v` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (vendor_organization_id, period_start, period_end, version)`

**Indexes**

- `fee_statements_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX fee_statements_pkey ON public.fee_statements USING btree (id)`
- `fee_statements_vendor_organization_id_period_start_period_end_v` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX fee_statements_vendor_organization_id_period_start_period_end_v ON public.fee_statements USING btree (vendor_organization_id, period_start, period_end, version)`

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `financial_snapshot_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Financial snapshot id. |
| `order_line_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Order line id. |
| `source_quantity` | `numeric` | No | — | Source quantity. |
| `source_unit_price_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Source unit price centavos. |
| `gross_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Gross centavos. |
| `discount_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Discount centavos. |
| `vat_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Vat centavos. |
| `ordinary_payable_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Ordinary payable centavos. |
| `principal_allocated_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Principal allocated centavos. |
| `refund_allocated_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Refund allocated centavos. |
| `tax_category` | `character varying` | No | — | Tax category. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |

**Constraints**

- `financial_snapshot_line_amounts_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (source_quantity > 0::numeric AND source_unit_price_centavos >= 0 AND gross_centavos >= 0 AND discount_centavos >= 0 AND discount_centavos <= gross_centavos AND vat_centavos >= 0 AND vat_centavos <= ordinary_payable_centavos AND ordinary_payable_centavos = (gross_centavos - discount_centavos) AND principal_allocated_centavos >= 0 AND refund_allocated_centavos >= 0 AND (principal_allocated_centavos + refund_allocated_centavos) <= ordinary_payable_centavos)`

## `financial_snapshots`

FIN-01–FIN-12 financial control and evidence record.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `order_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Order id. |
| `quotation_version_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Quotation version id. |
| `tax_rule_version_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Tax rule version id. |
| `environment` | `character varying` | No | `'TEST'::character varying` | Environment. |
| `materials_gross_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Materials gross centavos. |
| `vendor_discount_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Vendor discount centavos. |
| `materials_payable_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Materials payable centavos. |
| `materials_vat_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Materials vat centavos. |
| `materials_exclusive_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Materials exclusive centavos. |
| `delivery_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Delivery centavos. |
| `delivery_vat_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Delivery vat centavos. |
| `processing_fee_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Processing fee centavos. |
| `nrpc_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Nrpc centavos. |
| `buyer_total_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Buyer total centavos. |
| `currency` | `character varying` | No | `'PHP'::character varying` | Currency. |
| `fee_policy_version_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Fee policy version id. |
| `calculation_version` | `character varying` | No | — | Calculation version. |
| `rounding_allocation` | `jsonb` | No | — | Rounding allocation. |
| `calculation_hash` | `character varying` | No | — | Calculation hash. |
| `published_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | No | — | Published at. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |

**Constraints**

- `financial_snapshot_amounts_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (materials_gross_centavos >= 0 AND vendor_discount_centavos >= 0 AND materials_payable_centavos = (materials_gross_centavos - vendor_discount_centavos) AND materials_vat_centavos >= 0 AND materials_exclusive_centavos = (materials_payable_centavos - materials_vat_centavos) AND delivery_centavos >= 0 AND delivery_vat_centavos >= 0 AND delivery_vat_centavos <= delivery_centavos AND processing_fee_centavos >= 0 AND nrpc_centavos >= 0 AND nrpc_centavos <= materials_payable_centavos AND buyer_total_centavos = (materials_payable_centavos + delivery_centavos + processing_fee_centavos))`
- `financial_snapshot_environment_currency_check` — CHECK: `CHECK ((environment::text = ANY (ARRAY['TEST'::character varying, 'DEMO'::character varying, 'LIVE'::character varying]::text[])) AND currency::text = 'PHP'::text)`
- `financial_snapshots_fee_policy_version_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (fee_policy_version_id) REFERENCES fee_policy_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `financial_snapshots_order_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `financial_snapshots_quotation_version_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (quotation_version_id) REFERENCES quotation_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `financial_snapshots_tax_rule_version_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (tax_rule_version_id) REFERENCES tax_rule_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `financial_snapshots_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `financial_snapshots_calculation_hash_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (calculation_hash)`
- `financial_snapshots_order_id_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (order_id)`

**Indexes**

- `financial_snapshots_calculation_hash_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX financial_snapshots_calculation_hash_unique ON public.financial_snapshots USING btree (calculation_hash)`
- `financial_snapshots_order_id_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX financial_snapshots_order_id_unique ON public.financial_snapshots USING btree (order_id)`
- `financial_snapshots_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX financial_snapshots_pkey ON public.financial_snapshots USING btree (id)`

## `material_price_daily_aggregates`

Phase 1 platform foundation record.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `analytics_refresh_run_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Analytics refresh run id. |
| `material_comparable_group_version_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Material comparable group version id. |
| `environment` | `character varying` | No | — | Environment. |
| `dataset_id` | `character varying` | No | — | Dataset id. |
| `local_snapshot_date` | `date` | No | — | Local snapshot date. |
| `audience` | `character varying` | No | — | Audience. |
| `geography_scope_type` | `character varying` | No | — | Geography scope type. |
| `geography_scope_hash` | `character varying` | No | — | Geography scope hash. |
| `radius_km` | `smallint` | Yes | — | Radius km. |
| `distinct_vendor_count` | `integer` | No | — | Distinct vendor count. |
| `distinct_listing_count` | `integer` | No | — | Distinct listing count. |
| `average_php_price` | `numeric` | No | — | Average php price. |
| `minimum_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Minimum centavos. |
| `maximum_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Maximum centavos. |
| `composition_hash` | `character varying` | No | — | Composition hash. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |

**Constraints**

- `material_daily_aggregate_radius_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (geography_scope_type::text = 'RADIUS'::text AND (radius_km = ANY (ARRAY[5, 10, 20, 30, 40, 50])) OR geography_scope_type::text = 'PSGC'::text AND radius_km IS NULL)`
- `material_daily_aggregate_scope_check` — CHECK: `CHECK ((environment::text = ANY (ARRAY['TEST'::character varying, 'DEMO'::character varying]::text[])) AND (audience::text = ANY (ARRAY['BUYER'::character varying, 'VENDOR'::character varying, 'ADMIN'::character varying]::text[])) AND (geography_scope_type::text = ANY (ARRAY['RADIUS'::character varying, 'PSGC'::character varying]::text[])))`
- `material_daily_aggregate_values_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (distinct_vendor_count >= 1 AND distinct_listing_count >= 1 AND average_php_price > 0::numeric AND minimum_centavos > 0 AND maximum_centavos >= minimum_centavos AND (audience::text <> 'VENDOR'::text OR distinct_vendor_count >= 3))`
- `material_price_daily_aggregates_analytics_refresh_run_id_foreig` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (analytics_refresh_run_id) REFERENCES analytics_refresh_runs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `material_price_daily_aggregates_material_comparable_group_versi` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (material_comparable_group_version_id) REFERENCES material_comparable_group_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `material_price_daily_aggregates_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `material_daily_aggregate_scope_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (analytics_refresh_run_id, material_comparable_group_version_id, audience, geography_scope_hash, radius_km)`

**Indexes**

- `material_daily_aggregate_lookup_index` — `CREATE INDEX material_daily_aggregate_lookup_index ON public.material_price_daily_aggregates USING btree (environment, dataset_id, material_comparable_group_version_id, local_snapshot_date)`
- `material_daily_aggregate_null_safe_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX material_daily_aggregate_null_safe_unique ON public.material_price_daily_aggregates USING btree (analytics_refresh_run_id, material_comparable_group_version_id, audience, geography_scope_hash, COALESCE((radius_km)::integer, 0))`
- `material_daily_aggregate_scope_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX material_daily_aggregate_scope_unique ON public.material_price_daily_aggregates USING btree (analytics_refresh_run_id, material_comparable_group_version_id, audience, geography_scope_hash, radius_km)`
- `material_price_daily_aggregates_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX material_price_daily_aggregates_pkey ON public.material_price_daily_aggregates USING btree (id)`

- `materials_analytics_cache_scope_check` — CHECK: `CHECK ((environment::text = ANY (ARRAY['TEST'::character varying, 'DEMO'::character varying]::text[])) AND (audience::text = ANY (ARRAY['BUYER'::character varying, 'VENDOR'::character varying, 'ADMIN'::character varying]::text[])) AND (geography_scope_type::text = ANY (ARRAY['RADIUS'::character varying, 'PSGC'::character varying]::text[])))`
- `materials_analytics_cache_window_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (window_start <= window_end AND (window_end - window_start) <= 365 AND minimum_vendor_count >= 1 AND (audience::text <> 'VENDOR'::text OR minimum_vendor_count >= 3))`
- `materials_analytics_result_cache_analytics_refresh_run_id_forei` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (analytics_refresh_run_id) REFERENCES analytics_refresh_runs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `materials_analytics_result_cache_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `materials_analytics_cache_scope_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (analytics_refresh_run_id, audience, geography_scope_hash, radius_km, window_start, window_end, filters_hash)`

**Indexes**

- `materials_analytics_cache_expiry_index` — `CREATE INDEX materials_analytics_cache_expiry_index ON public.materials_analytics_result_cache USING btree (environment, dataset_id, audience, expires_at)`
- `materials_analytics_cache_null_safe_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX materials_analytics_cache_null_safe_unique ON public.materials_analytics_result_cache USING btree (analytics_refresh_run_id, audience, geography_scope_hash, COALESCE((radius_km)::integer, 0), window_start, window_end, filters_hash)`
- `materials_analytics_cache_scope_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX materials_analytics_cache_scope_unique ON public.materials_analytics_result_cache USING btree (analytics_refresh_run_id, audience, geography_scope_hash, radius_km, window_start, window_end, filters_hash)`
- `materials_analytics_result_cache_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX materials_analytics_result_cache_pkey ON public.materials_analytics_result_cache USING btree (id)`

## `payments`

Payment, refund, or physical-money evidence record with an independent lifecycle.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `order_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Order id. |
| `fee_statement_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Fee statement id. |
| `purpose` | `character varying` | No | — | Purpose. |
| `environment` | `character varying` | No | `'TEST'::character varying` | Environment. |
| `provider` | `character varying` | No | `'XENDIT'::character varying` | Provider. |
| `provider_reference` | `character varying` | Yes | — | Provider reference. |
| `principal_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Principal centavos. |
| `processing_fee_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Processing fee centavos. |
| `total_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Total centavos. |
| `currency` | `character` | No | `'PHP'::bpchar` | Currency. |
| `state` | `character varying` | No | `'PENDING'::character varying` | State. |
| `idempotency_key` | `character varying` | No | — | Idempotency key. |
| `expires_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Expires at. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |

**Constraints**

- `payment_amounts_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (principal_centavos >= 0 AND processing_fee_centavos >= 0 AND total_centavos = (principal_centavos + processing_fee_centavos))`
- `payments_purpose_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (purpose::text = ANY (ARRAY['FULL_ORDER_PAYMENT'::character varying, 'NRPC_ASSURANCE_PAYMENT'::character varying, 'ORDER_BALANCE_PAYMENT'::character varying, 'PLATFORM_FEE_PAYMENT'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `payments_fee_statement_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (fee_statement_id) REFERENCES fee_statements(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `payments_order_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `payments_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `payments_idempotency_key_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (idempotency_key)`
- `payments_provider_reference_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (provider_reference)`

**Indexes**

- `payments_idempotency_key_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payments_idempotency_key_unique ON public.payments USING btree (idempotency_key)`
- `payments_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payments_pkey ON public.payments USING btree (id)`
- `payments_provider_reference_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payments_provider_reference_unique ON public.payments USING btree (provider_reference)`

## `physical_payment_records`

Payment, refund, or physical-money evidence record with an independent lifecycle.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `order_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Order id. |
| `recorded_by_user_id` | `bigint` | No | — | Recorded by user id. |
| `record_kind` | `character varying` | No | `'COLLECTION'::character varying` | Record kind. |
| `method` | `character varying` | No | — | Method. |
| `obligation_before_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Obligation before centavos. |
| `amount_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Amount centavos. |
| `remaining_obligation_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Remaining obligation centavos. |
| `state` | `character varying` | No | — | State. |
| `evidence_file_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Evidence file id. |
| `original_record_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Original record id. |
| `correction_reason` | `text` | Yes | — | Correction reason. |
| `idempotency_key` | `character varying` | No | — | Idempotency key. |
| `recorded_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | No | — | Recorded at. |
| `buyer_acknowledged_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Buyer acknowledged at. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |

**Constraints**

- `physical_payment_amount_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (obligation_before_centavos >= 0 AND amount_centavos >= 0 AND remaining_obligation_centavos >= 0 AND amount_centavos <= obligation_before_centavos AND remaining_obligation_centavos = (obligation_before_centavos - amount_centavos))`
- `physical_payment_correction_link_check` — CHECK: `CHECK ((record_kind::text = 'CORRECTION'::text) = (original_record_id IS NOT NULL AND correction_reason IS NOT NULL))`
- `physical_payment_evidence_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (amount_centavos = 0 OR evidence_file_id IS NOT NULL)`
- `physical_payment_kind_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (record_kind::text = ANY (ARRAY['COLLECTION'::character varying, 'CORRECTION'::character varying, 'CANCELLATION_RELEASE'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `physical_payment_state_amount_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (state::text = 'UNPAID'::text AND amount_centavos = 0 AND remaining_obligation_centavos = obligation_before_centavos OR state::text = 'PARTIALLY_RECORDED'::text AND amount_centavos > 0 AND remaining_obligation_centavos > 0 OR state::text = 'PHYSICAL_PAYMENT_RECORDED'::text AND amount_centavos > 0 AND remaining_obligation_centavos = 0 OR state::text = 'CANCELLED_UNPAID'::text AND record_kind::text = 'CANCELLATION_RELEASE'::text AND amount_centavos = 0 AND remaining_obligation_centavos = obligation_before_centavos)`
- `physical_payment_state_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (state::text = ANY (ARRAY['UNPAID'::character varying, 'PARTIALLY_RECORDED'::character varying, 'PHYSICAL_PAYMENT_RECORDED'::character varying, 'CANCELLED_UNPAID'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `physical_payment_records_evidence_file_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (evidence_file_id) REFERENCES files(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `physical_payment_records_order_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `physical_payment_records_original_record_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (original_record_id) REFERENCES physical_payment_records(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `physical_payment_records_recorded_by_user_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (recorded_by_user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `physical_payment_records_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `physical_payment_records_idempotency_key_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (idempotency_key)`

**Indexes**

- `physical_payment_records_idempotency_key_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX physical_payment_records_idempotency_key_unique ON public.physical_payment_records USING btree (idempotency_key)`
- `physical_payment_records_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX physical_payment_records_pkey ON public.physical_payment_records USING btree (id)`

MAT-01–MAT-07 comparability, observation, publication, or audience-safe aggregate record.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `environment` | `character varying` | No | `'TEST'::character varying` | Environment. |
| `dataset_id` | `character varying` | No | — | Dataset id. |
| `vendor_organization_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Vendor organization id. |
| `listing_variant_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Listing variant id. |
| `listing_price_version_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Listing price version id. |

| `local_snapshot_date` | `date` | No | — | Local snapshot date. |
| `ordinary_payable_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Ordinary payable centavos. |
| `normalized_php_price` | `numeric` | No | — | Normalized php price. |
| `tax_category` | `character varying` | No | — | Tax category. |
| `address_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Address id. |
| `address_version` | `integer` | No | — | Address version. |
| `eligibility_version` | `character varying` | No | — | Eligibility version. |

**Constraints**

- `price_observation_address_version_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (address_version >= 1)`
- `price_observation_amount_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (ordinary_payable_centavos > 0 AND normalized_php_price > 0::numeric)`
- `price_observation_date_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (local_snapshot_date = (observed_at AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Manila'::text)::date)`
- `price_observation_environment_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (environment::text = ANY (ARRAY['TEST'::character varying, 'DEMO'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `price_observation_selection_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (selected_for_average = false OR comparable_group_version_id IS NOT NULL AND exclusion_reason IS NULL)`
- `price_observation_source_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (source_kind::text = 'PUBLIC_LISTING'::text)`
- `price_observations_address_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (address_id) REFERENCES addresses(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `price_observations_analytics_refresh_run_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (analytics_refresh_run_id) REFERENCES analytics_refresh_runs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `price_observations_comparable_group_version_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (comparable_group_version_id) REFERENCES material_comparable_group_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `price_observations_listing_price_version_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (listing_price_version_id) REFERENCES listing_price_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `price_observations_listing_variant_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (listing_variant_id) REFERENCES listing_variants(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `price_observations_vendor_organization_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (vendor_organization_id) REFERENCES vendor_organizations(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `price_observations_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `price_observations_analytics_refresh_run_id_listing_variant_id_` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (analytics_refresh_run_id, listing_variant_id)`

**Indexes**

- `price_observations_analytics_refresh_run_id_listing_variant_id_` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX price_observations_analytics_refresh_run_id_listing_variant_id_ ON public.price_observations USING btree (analytics_refresh_run_id, listing_variant_id)`
- `price_observations_environment_dataset_id_local_snapshot_date_i` — `CREATE INDEX price_observations_environment_dataset_id_local_snapshot_date_i ON public.price_observations USING btree (environment, dataset_id, local_snapshot_date)`
- `price_observations_geography_gist` — `CREATE INDEX price_observations_geography_gist ON public.price_observations USING gist (recorded_store_geography)`
- `price_observations_group_date_index` — `CREATE INDEX price_observations_group_date_index ON public.price_observations USING btree (environment, dataset_id, comparable_group_version_id, local_snapshot_date)`
- `price_observations_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX price_observations_pkey ON public.price_observations USING btree (id)`
- `price_observations_selected_vendor_group_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX price_observations_selected_vendor_group_unique ON public.price_observations USING btree (analytics_refresh_run_id, vendor_organization_id, comparable_group_version_id) WHERE (selected_for_average = true)`

## `refunds`

Payment, refund, or physical-money evidence record with an independent lifecycle.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `target_type` | `character varying` | No | `'ORDER'::character varying` | Target type. |
| `order_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Order id. |
| `fee_statement_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Fee statement id. |
| `source_payment_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Source payment id. |
| `fee_adjustment_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Fee adjustment id. |
| `trigger` | `character varying` | No | — | Trigger. |
| `amount_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Amount centavos. |
| `source_captured_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Source captured centavos. |
| `prior_allocated_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Prior allocated centavos. |
| `state` | `character varying` | No | `'REFUND_PENDING'::character varying` | State. |
| `idempotency_key` | `character varying` | No | — | Idempotency key. |
| `provider_reference` | `character varying` | Yes | — | Provider reference. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |

**Constraints**

- `refund_amount_positive_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (amount_centavos > 0)`
- `refund_source_allocation_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (source_captured_centavos >= 0 AND prior_allocated_centavos >= 0 AND prior_allocated_centavos <= source_captured_centavos AND amount_centavos <= (source_captured_centavos - prior_allocated_centavos))`
- `refund_target_trigger_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (target_type::text = 'ORDER'::text AND (trigger::text = ANY (ARRAY['CANCELLATION'::character varying, 'DISPUTE_CONCLUSION'::character varying, 'TECHNICAL_COMPENSATION'::character varying]::text[])) AND order_id IS NOT NULL OR target_type::text = 'PLATFORM_FEE'::text AND trigger::text = 'FEE_CREDIT'::text AND fee_statement_id IS NOT NULL AND fee_adjustment_id IS NOT NULL)`
- `refunds_fee_adjustment_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (fee_adjustment_id) REFERENCES fee_adjustments(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `refunds_fee_statement_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (fee_statement_id) REFERENCES fee_statements(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `refunds_order_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `refunds_source_payment_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (source_payment_id) REFERENCES payments(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `refunds_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `refunds_idempotency_key_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (idempotency_key)`
- `refunds_provider_reference_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (provider_reference)`

**Indexes**

- `refunds_idempotency_key_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX refunds_idempotency_key_unique ON public.refunds USING btree (idempotency_key)`
- `refunds_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX refunds_pkey ON public.refunds USING btree (id)`
- `refunds_provider_reference_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX refunds_provider_reference_unique ON public.refunds USING btree (provider_reference)`

## `remittance_assessments`

FIN-01–FIN-12 financial control and evidence record.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `remittance_group_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Remittance group id. |
| `financial_snapshot_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Financial snapshot id. |
| `order_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Order id. |
| `environment` | `character varying` | No | `'TEST'::character varying` | Environment. |
| `taxpayer_key_hash` | `character varying` | No | — | Taxpayer key hash. |
| `obligation_type` | `character varying` | No | — | Obligation type. |
| `assigned_withholding_entity` | `character varying` | No | — | Assigned withholding entity. |
| `collections_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Collections centavos. |
| `refunds_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Refunds centavos. |
| `discounts_refunded_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Discounts refunded centavos. |
| `vat_refunded_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Vat refunded centavos. |
| `delivery_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Delivery centavos. |
| `vat_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Vat centavos. |
| `provider_charge_centavos` | `bigint` | No | `'0'::bigint` | Integer Philippine centavos. Provider charge centavos. |
| `principal_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Principal centavos. |
| `gross_basis_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Gross basis centavos. |
| `withheld_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Withheld centavos. |
| `threshold_before_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Threshold before centavos. |
| `threshold_after_centavos` | `bigint` | No | — | Integer Philippine centavos. Threshold after centavos. |
| `effective_rate_basis_points` | `integer` | No | — | Effective rate basis points. |
| `threshold_status` | `character varying` | No | — | Threshold status. |
| `calculation_state` | `character varying` | No | `'CALCULATED'::character varying` | Calculation state. |
| `deduction_evidence_state` | `character varying` | No | `'UNCONFIRMED'::character varying` | Deduction evidence state. |
| `reconciliation_state` | `character varying` | No | `'PENDING'::character varying` | Reconciliation state. |
| `evidence_file_id` | `uuid` | Yes | — | Evidence file id. |
| `assessment_reason` | `text` | No | — | Assessment reason. |
| `calculation` | `jsonb` | No | — | Calculation. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |

**Constraints**

- `remittance_amounts_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (collections_centavos >= 0 AND refunds_centavos >= 0 AND refunds_centavos <= collections_centavos AND discounts_refunded_centavos >= 0 AND vat_refunded_centavos >= 0 AND delivery_centavos >= 0 AND vat_centavos >= 0 AND provider_charge_centavos >= 0 AND principal_centavos >= 0 AND gross_basis_centavos >= 0 AND withheld_centavos >= 0 AND withheld_centavos <= gross_basis_centavos AND threshold_before_centavos >= 0 AND threshold_after_centavos >= threshold_before_centavos AND effective_rate_basis_points >= 0 AND effective_rate_basis_points <= 10000)`
- `remittance_calculation_state_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (calculation_state::text = ANY (ARRAY['CALCULATED'::character varying, 'POSTED'::character varying, 'BASE_REVIEW_REQUIRED'::character varying, 'VOIDED'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `remittance_deduction_state_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (deduction_evidence_state::text = ANY (ARRAY['UNCONFIRMED'::character varying, 'SIMULATED_WITHHELD'::character varying, 'PROVIDER_REPORTED'::character varying, 'PLATFORM_EVIDENCED'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `remittance_environment_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (environment::text = ANY (ARRAY['TEST'::character varying, 'DEMO'::character varying, 'LIVE'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `remittance_reconciliation_state_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (reconciliation_state::text = ANY (ARRAY['PENDING'::character varying, 'RECONCILED'::character varying, 'RECONCILIATION_EXCEPTION'::character varying]::text[]))`
- `remittance_assessments_evidence_file_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (evidence_file_id) REFERENCES files(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `remittance_assessments_financial_snapshot_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (financial_snapshot_id) REFERENCES financial_snapshots(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `remittance_assessments_order_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `remittance_assessments_remittance_group_id_foreign` — FOREIGN KEY: `FOREIGN KEY (remittance_group_id) REFERENCES remittance_groups(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT`
- `remittance_assessments_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `remittance_assessment_scope_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (environment, taxpayer_key_hash, remittance_group_id, obligation_type)`

**Indexes**

- `remittance_assessment_scope_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX remittance_assessment_scope_unique ON public.remittance_assessments USING btree (environment, taxpayer_key_hash, remittance_group_id, obligation_type)`
- `remittance_assessments_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX remittance_assessments_pkey ON public.remittance_assessments USING btree (id)`

## `vendor_memberships`

Vendor organization, access, onboarding, or storefront record.

| Column | Database type | Null | Default | Key / meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `id` | `uuid` | No | — | Primary key. Id. |
| `vendor_organization_id` | `uuid` | No | — | Vendor organization id. |
| `user_id` | `bigint` | No | — | User id. |
| `role` | `character varying` | No | — | Role. |
| `can_manage_staff` | `boolean` | No | `false` | Can manage staff. |
| `status` | `character varying` | No | `'ACTIVE'::character varying` | Status. |
| `activated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Activated at. |
| `deactivated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Deactivated at. |
| `created_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Created at. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamp with time zone` | Yes | — | Updated at. |

**Constraints**

- `vendor_manager_delegation_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (can_manage_staff = false OR role::text = 'STORE_MANAGER'::text)`
- `vendor_memberships_role_check` — CHECK: `CHECK (role::text = ANY (ARRAY['OWNER'::character varying, 'STORE_MANAGER'::character varying, 'STORE_STAFF'::character varying, 'CUSTOMER_SERVICE'::character varying, 'INVENTORY'::character varying, 'FULFILLMENT'::character varying]::text[]))`

- `vendor_memberships_pkey` — PRIMARY KEY: `PRIMARY KEY (id)`
- `vendor_memberships_user_id_unique` — UNIQUE: `UNIQUE (user_id)`

**Indexes**

- `vendor_memberships_pkey` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX vendor_memberships_pkey ON public.vendor_memberships USING btree (id)`
- `vendor_memberships_user_id_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX vendor_memberships_user_id_unique ON public.vendor_memberships USING btree (user_id)`
- `vendor_memberships_vendor_organization_id_role_status_index` — `CREATE INDEX vendor_memberships_vendor_organization_id_role_status_index ON public.vendor_memberships USING btree (vendor_organization_id, role, status)`
- `vendor_one_active_owner_unique` — `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX vendor_one_active_owner_unique ON public.vendor_memberships USING btree (vendor_organization_id) WHERE (((role)::text = 'OWNER'::text) AND ((status)::text = 'ACTIVE'::text))`
